Runs in Node or a browser.\n * This is the cheapest possible check of a decision the whole block layer rests on.\n *\n *   node spike/ge-bench.mjs              # default sweep\n *   node spike/ge-bench.mjs 768 256      # one (K, L)\n *\n * In a browser: import it and call run(K, L) — same code path, and the browser\n * number is the one that matters. Node and Chrome share V8, but a phone is the\n * target; expect roughly 3–5x slower than a desktop.\n *\n * WHAT IT MEASURES\n * It performs a complete GF(2) Gaussian elimination decode of one block — the\n * real inner loop, not a synthetic XOR microbenchmark — and reports the sustained\n * XOR throughput achieved. Compare that against the \"required\" figure printed by\n * docs/research/sim/ge_cost_model.py for the same (K, L) and wire rate.\n */\n\nconst HEADER = 13;\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------- prng (mulberry32)\nfunction prng(seed) {\n  let a = seed >>> 0;\n  return () => {\n    a = (a + 0x6d2b79f5) >>> 0;\n    let t = a;\n    t = Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 15), t | 1);\n    t ^= t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), t | 61);\n    return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;\n  };\n}\n\n/** Harmonic Pr(d) ∝ 1/d truncated at `cap` — D25. */\nfunction degreeSampler(K, cap, rnd) {\n  const hi = Math.min(cap ?? K, K);\n  const cum = new Float64Array(hi);\n  let total = 0;\n  for (let d = 1; d <= hi; d++) total += 1 / d;\n  let acc = 0;\n  for (let d = 1; d <= hi; d++) { acc += 1 / d / total; cum[d - 1] = acc; }\n  return () => {\n    const r = rnd();\n    let lo = 0, high = hi - 1;\n    while (lo < high) { const mid = (lo + high) >> 1; if (cum[mid] < r) lo = mid + 1; else high = mid; }\n    return lo + 1;\n  };\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------- the decode\nexport function run(K, L, { cap = 64, seed = 0xC0FFEE } = {}) {\n  const MASKW = Math.ceil(K / 32);\n  const PAYW = Math.ceil(L / 4);\n  const ROWB = MASKW * 4 + PAYW * 4;      // bytes touched per row operation\n\n  // Pivot store: index = pivot bit position.\n  const pivMask = new Array(K).fill(null);\n  const pivPay = new Array(K).fill(null);\n\n  const rnd = prng(seed);\n  const degree = degreeSampler(K, cap, rnd);\n  const idx = new Int32Array(K);\n\n  let rank = 0, packets = 0, rowOps = 0;\n  const mask = new Uint32Array(MASKW);\n  const pay = new Uint32Array(PAYW);\n\n  const t0 = performance.now();\n\n  while (rank < K) {\n    // --- build one encoded packet\n    mask.fill(0);\n    for (let i = 0; i < PAYW; i++) pay[i] = (rnd() * 4294967296) >>> 0;\n    const d = degree();\n    // sample d distinct indices (partial Fisher–Yates over a reused scratch)\n    for (let i = 0; i < K; i++) idx[i] = i;\n    for (let i = 0; i < d; i++) {\n      const j = i + ((rnd() * (K - i)) | 0);\n      const tmp = idx[i]; idx[i] = idx[j]; idx[j] = tmp;\n      mask[idx[i] >>> 5] ^= 1 << (idx[i] & 31);\n    }\n    packets++;\n\n    // --- reduce against stored pivots\n    let w = MASKW - 1;\n    for (;;) {\n      while (w >= 0 && mask[w] === 0) w--;\n      if (w < 0) break;                                   // reduced to zero: dependent\n      const bit = 31 - Math.clz32(mask[w]);\n      const p = (w << 5) | bit;\n\n      const pm = pivMask[p];\n      if (pm === null) {                                  // new pivot\n        pivMask[p] = mask.slice();\n        pivPay[p] = pay.slice();\n        rank++;\n        break;\n      }\n      const pp = pivPay[p];\n      for (let i = 0; i <= w; i++) mask[i] ^= pm[i];\n      for (let i = 0; i < PAYW; i++) pay[i] ^= pp[i];\n      rowOps++;\n    }\n  }\n\n  const ms = performance.now() - t0;\n  const bytes = rowOps * ROWB;\n  return {\n    K, L, cap, packets, rowOps, ms,\n    overheadPct: ((packets - K) / K) * 100,\n    xorBytes: bytes,\n    throughputMBs: bytes / 1e6 / (ms / 1000),\n    blockBytes: K * L,\n    matrixBytes: (K * K) / 8,\n  };\n}\n\n/** Sustained XOR the decoder must achieve to keep pace — matches ge_cost_model.py. */\nexport const requiredMBs = (K, L, wireBytesPerSec) =>\n  (K * (K / 8 + L) * wireBytesPerSec) / L / 1e6;\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------- report\nconst STAGES = [[\"Stage 1\", 30], [\"Stage 2\", 60], [\"Stage 3\", 106]];\nconst BUDGET = 200; Files on a CDN, works offline, auditable once and trusted\n--\nitself an unmeasured estimate (§18 R1). Margin at Stage 3: **1.74×**.\n\n| K | Block | Stage 1 | Stage 2 | Stage 3 | |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| 512 | 128.0 KB | 19.7 MB/s | 39.3 MB/s | 69.5 MB/s | 2.88× margin |\n| **768** | **192.0 KB** | **32.4 MB/s** | **64.9 MB/s** | **114.6 MB/s** | **adopted, 1.74× margin** |\n| 1024 | 256.0 KB | 47.2 MB/s | 94.4 MB/s | 166.7 MB/s | 1.20× margin |\n| 1152 | 288.0 KB | 55.3 MB/s | 110.6 MB/s | 195.4 MB/s | 1.02× — the ceiling |\n| 1536 | 384.0 KB | 82.6 MB/s | 165.2 MB/s | 291.8 MB/s | 1.5× **over** at Stage 3 |\n\n### 3.1.1 L is set by the *conservative* ladder rung, not the nominal one\n\nA revision of this plan briefly set L = 507 B (\"one packet per v15 tile\") and broke\nD16's ladder. D15's source note states the governing rule plainly: **pick L to fit the\nsmallest tile any profile would use.** At L = 507 the packet is 520 B and the\nconservative rung, v10-L, holds **271 B** — so it could carry **zero** packets, only\nbeacons. That destroys the ladder's one load-bearing property: *a mis-guessed profile\nyields zero; a ladder always yields something.*\n\n**Rungs are therefore defined by packet count, with the QR version chosen to fit** —\nnot the reverse. At L = 256 B, packet = 13 + 256 = **269 B**:\n--\n| R1 conservative | 1 | 269 B | v10-L | 271 B | 2 B |\n| R2 nominal | 2 | 538 B | v16-L | 586 B | 48 B |\n| R3 aggressive | 3 | 807 B | v20-L | 858 B | 51 B |\n| R4 (probe only) | 4 | 1076 B | v23-L | 1091 B | 15 B |\n\nWaste stays ≤ 51 B on every rung. The pressure that motivated raising L has also\ngone: at K = 768 the mask term is K/8 = 96 B against a 256 B payload (27%), not the\n8× dominance that made L = 256 pathological at K = 16,384.\n\n**A rung MUST carry a whole number of packets.** Partial packets across tiles would\ncouple tiles together and destroy the independence D1 depends on.\n\n### 3.2 Neither side can hold the file in memory\n\nA multi-GB `Uint8Array` is not allocatable (Chrome's `ArrayBuffer` ceiling is ~2 GB;\nSafari's is lower), and would get a phone tab killed regardless.","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false}}
